in (
9.42
), we obtain

\begin{split}\|P_{k}dh_{j},P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&C2^{-\delta_{1}k}\cdot(1+M_{0})^{3}2^{-7\delta_{1}j},\quad k\geq j,\end{split}

(9.43)

Case 2: low frequency(
k<j
).
In this case, it’s much different from the high frequency. Fortunately, there is some good estimates for difference terms
P_{k}(d{h}_{m+1}-d{h}_{m})
and
P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{m})
. Following (
1.18
), we set

\begin{split}&\mathbf{v}_{m}=\mathbf{v}_{+m}+\mathbf{v}_{-m},\\
&\mathbf{v}_{\pm m}=(v^{0}_{\pm m},v^{1}_{\pm m},v^{2}_{\pm m}),\\
&(\mathbf{Id}-\mathbf{{P}})v^{\alpha}_{-m}=\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma}\partial_{\beta}w_{m\gamma}.\end{split}

We will obtain some good estimates of
P_{k}(d{h}_{m+1}-d{h}_{m})
and
P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{m})
by using (
9.39
)-(
9.40
). Indeed, this good estimate is from a loss of derivatives of Strichartz estimate. We claim that

\displaystyle\|h_{m+1}-h_{m},\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-\mathbf{v}_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{2}_{x}}\leq C2^{-(s-\delta_{1})m}M_{0},

(9.44)

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{w}_{m+1}-\mathbf{w}_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{2}_{x}}\leq C2^{-(\frac{3}{2}-\delta_{1})m}M_{0}.

(9.45)

To verify (
9.44
) and (
9.45
), we start from the initial data. Applying Bernstein’s inequality, we get

\begin{split}\|{\mathbf{v}}_{0(m+1)}-{\mathbf{v}}_{0m}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim&2^{-sm}\|{\mathbf{v}}_{0(m+1)}-{\mathbf{v}}_{0m}\|_{\dot{H}^{s}}.\end{split}

(9.46)

Similarly, we obtain

\begin{split}&\|{h}_{0(m+1)}-{h}_{0m}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-sm}\|{h}_{0(m+1)}-{h}_{0m}\|_{\dot{H}^{s}},\\
&\|{\mathbf{w}}_{0(m+1)}-{\mathbf{w}}_{0m}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-{\frac{3}{2}}m}\|{\mathbf{w}}_{0(m+1)}-{\mathbf{w}}_{0m}\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{3}{2}}}.\end{split}

(9.47)

Based on (
9.46
) and (
9.47
), we can use the structure of the system (
1.1
) to prove (
9.44
)-(
9.45
). Let
{\mathbf{U}}_{m}=(p(h_{m}),\mathrm{e}^{-h_{m}}\mathbf{v}_{m})^{\mathrm{T}}
. Then
{\mathbf{U}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{U}}_{m}
satisfies

\begin{cases}&A^{0}({\mathbf{U}}_{m+1})\partial_{t}({\mathbf{U}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{U}}_{m})+A^{i}({\mathbf{U}}_{m+1})\partial_{i}({\mathbf{U}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{U}}_{m})=\Pi_{m},\\
&({\mathbf{U}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{U}}_{m})|_{t=0}={\mathbf{U}}_{0(m+1)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{0m},\end{cases}

(9.48)

where

\Pi_{m}=-[A^{0}({\mathbf{U}}_{m+1})-A^{0}({\mathbf{U}}_{m})]\partial_{t}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}-[A^{i}({\mathbf{U}}_{m+1})-A^{i}({\mathbf{U}}_{m})]\partial_{i}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}.

(9.49)

From (
9.49
), we can see

|\Pi_{m}|\lesssim|{\mathbf{U}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{U}}_{m}|\cdot|d{\mathbf{U}}_{m}|.

Multiplying
{\mathbf{U}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{U}}_{m}
on (
9.48
) and integrating it on
\mathbb{R}^{2}
, we have

\frac{d}{dt}\|{\mathbf{U}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{U}}_{m}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\|(d{\mathbf{U}}_{m+1},d{\mathbf{U}}_{m})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|{\mathbf{U}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{U}}_{m}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}.

(9.50)

Integrating (
9.50
) on
[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]
, using Gronwall’s inequality and Strichartz estimate (
9.38
), it yields

\begin{split}\|{\mathbf{U}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{U}}_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim&\|{\mathbf{U}}_{0(m+1)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{0m}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}.\end{split}

Due to (
9.46
) and (
9.47
), we then have

\begin{split}\|{\mathbf{U}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{U}}_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{2}_{x}}\leq C2^{-(s-\delta_{1})m}M_{0}.\end{split}

By using Lemma
3.8
, we show that

\begin{split}\|{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m},{\rho}_{m+1}-{\rho}_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{2}_{x}}\leq C2^{-(s-\delta_{1})m}M_{0}.\end{split}

Thus, the estimate (
9.44
) holds. To prove (
9.45
), we consider the transport equation of
{\mathbf{w}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{m}
:

\begin{split}&\partial_{t}({w}^{\alpha}_{m+1}-{w}^{\alpha}_{m})+({v}^{0}_{m+1})^{-1}{v}^{i}_{m+1}\partial_{i}({w}^{\alpha}_{m+1}-{w}^{\alpha}_{m})\\
=&-\left\{(v^{0}_{m+1})^{-1}v^{i}_{m+1}-(v^{0}_{m})^{-1}v^{i}_{m}\right\}\partial_{i}w^{\alpha}_{m}+\left\{(v^{0}_{m+1})^{-1}w^{\kappa}_{{m+1}}-(v^{0}_{l})^{-1}w^{\kappa}_{m}\right\}\partial^{\alpha}v_{({m+1})\kappa}\\
&+(v^{0}_{m})^{-1}w^{\kappa}_{l}\partial^{\alpha}(v_{(m+1)\kappa}-v_{m\kappa})-\left\{(v^{0}_{m+1})^{-1}w^{\alpha}_{j}-(v^{0}_{l})^{-1}w^{\alpha}_{m}\right\}\partial_{\kappa}v^{\kappa}_{m+1}\\
&-(v^{0}_{l})^{-1}w^{\alpha}_{m}\partial_{\kappa}(v^{\kappa}_{m+1}-v^{\kappa}_{m})\end{split}

(9.51)

Multiplying with
{w}_{(m+1)\alpha}-{w}_{m\alpha}
on (
9.51
) and integrating it on
[0,t]\times\mathbb{R}^{2}
, we derive

\begin{split}\|{\mathbf{w}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{m}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\leq&\|{\mathbf{w}}_{0(m+1)}-{\mathbf{w}}_{0m}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+C\int^{t}_{0}\|\nabla{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|{\mathbf{w}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{m}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m}\|_{L^{\frac{8}{3}}_{x}}\|{\mathbf{w}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{m}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\nabla{\mathbf{w}}_{m}\|_{L^{8}_{x}}d\tau\\
\leq&\|{\mathbf{w}}_{0(m+1)}-{\mathbf{w}}_{0m}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+C\int^{t}_{0}\|\nabla{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|{\mathbf{w}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{m}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{4}}_{x}}\|{\mathbf{w}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{m}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\nabla{\mathbf{w}}_{m}\|_{L^{8}_{x}}d\tau.\end{split}

(9.52)

We also note that

\|{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{4}}_{x}}\lesssim\|{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m}\|^{1-\frac{1}{4s}}_{L^{2}_{x}}\|{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m}\|^{\frac{1}{4s}}_{H^{s}_{x}}.

(9.53)

Taking advantage of (
9.47
), (
9.44
), (
9.41
), (
9.52
), and (
9.53
), and using Gronwall’s inequality gives

\begin{split}\|{\mathbf{w}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim 2^{-(\frac{3}{2}-\delta_{1})m}M_{0}.\end{split}

This implies that (
9.45
) holds. On the other hand, for
\delta_{1}<s-\frac{7}{4}
, using Strichartz estimates (
9.40
)(similar to (
7.5
)), it yields

\begin{split}&\|\nabla({h}_{m+1}-{h}_{m})\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}+\|\nabla({\mathbf{v}}_{+(m+1)}-{\mathbf{v}}_{+m})\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}\\
\leq&C\|{h}_{m+1}-{h}_{m},{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}H^{\frac{7}{4}+2\delta_{1}}_{x}}+\|{\mathbf{w}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}H^{1+2\delta_{1}}_{x}}.\end{split}

(9.54)

Noting
s=2+10\delta_{1}
and using (
9.44
)-(
9.45
), we can bound (
9.54
) by

\begin{split}&\|\nabla({h}_{m+1}-{h}_{m})\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}+\|\nabla({\mathbf{v}}_{+(m+1)}-{\mathbf{v}}_{+m})\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}\leq C2^{-7\delta_{1}m}M_{0}.\end{split}

(9.55)

Applying (
9.45
) and Sobolev imbeddings, we can establish

\begin{split}\|\nabla({\mathbf{v}}_{-(m+1)}-{\mathbf{v}}_{-m})\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}\leq&\|\nabla({\mathbf{v}}_{-(m+1)}-{\mathbf{v}}_{-m})\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}\\
\leq&C\|{\mathbf{w}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}H^{1+2\delta_{1}}_{x}}\\
\leq&C2^{-7\delta_{1}m}(1+M_{0})^{2}.\end{split}

(9.56)

Adding (
9.55
) and (
9.56
), it yields

\begin{split}&\|\nabla({h}_{m+1}-{h}_{m})\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}+\|\nabla({\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}\leq C2^{-7\delta_{1}m}(1+M_{0})^{2}.\end{split}

(9.57)

By using (
1.1
) and (
9.57
), we obtain

\begin{split}&\|\partial_{t}({h}_{m+1}-{h}_{m})\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}+\|\partial_{t}({\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}\\
\leq&\|\nabla({h}_{m+1}-{h}_{m}),\nabla({\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}\cdot(1+\|\mathbf{v}_{m},h_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}H^{s}_{x}})\\
\leq&C(1+M_{0})^{3}2^{-6\delta_{1}m}.\end{split}

(9.58)

Due to (
9.57
) and (
9.58
), for
k<m
, we get

\|P_{k}d({h}_{m+1}-{h}_{m}),P_{k}d({\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq C2^{-\delta_{1}k}\cdot 2^{-6\delta_{1}m}(1+M_{0})^{3},

(9.59)

and

\|P_{k}d({h}_{m+1}-{h}_{m}),P_{k}d({\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq C2^{-\delta_{1}k}\cdot 2^{-6\delta_{1}m}(1+M_{0})^{3}.

9.3.3.
Uniform energy and Strichartz estimates on a regular time-interval
[0,T_{N_{0}}^{*}]
.

Recall (
9.22
), then
T_{N_{0}}^{*}=2^{-\delta_{1}N_{0}}(CM_{0})^{-3}
. Recall (
9.43
) and (
9.59
). Therefore, when
k\geq j
and
j\geq N_{0}
, using (
9.43
) and Hölder’s inequality, we have

\begin{split}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}dh_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&(T^{*}_{j})^{\frac{3}{4}}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}dh_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot C(1+M_{0})^{3}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}j}.\end{split}

(9.60)

When
k<j
and
m\geq N_{0}
, using (
9.59
) and Hölder’s inequality, we obtain

\|P_{k}d({h}_{m+1}-{h}_{m}),P_{k}d({\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot C(1+M_{0})^{3}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}m}.

(9.61)

On the other side, when
k\geq j
and
j<N_{0}
, using (
9.43
), we get

\begin{split}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}dh_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}dh_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}dh_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot C(1+M_{0})^{3}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}j}.\end{split}

(9.62)

When
k<j
and
m<N_{0}
, using (
9.59
) and Hölder’s inequality, it yields

\begin{split}&\|P_{k}d({h}_{j+1}-{h}_{j}),P_{k}d({\mathbf{v}}_{j+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{j})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\|P_{k}d({h}_{j+1}-{h}_{j}),P_{k}d({\mathbf{v}}_{j+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{j})\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\|P_{k}d({h}_{j+1}-{h}_{j}),P_{k}d({\mathbf{v}}_{j+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{j})\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{j+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot C(1+M_{0})^{3}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}j}.\end{split}

(9.63)

Due to a different time-interval for the sequence
(h_{j},\mathbf{v}_{j},w_{j})
, we will discuss the solutions
(h_{j},\mathbf{v}_{j},w_{j})
if
j\leq N_{0}
or
j\geq N_{0}+1
as follows.

Case 1:
j\leq N_{0}
.
In this case,
(h_{j},\mathbf{v}_{j},w_{j})
exists on
[0,T_{j}^{*}]
, and
[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]\subseteq[0,T^{*}_{j}]
. As a result, we don’t need to extend solutions
(h_{j},\mathbf{v}_{j},w_{j})
if
j\leq N_{0}
. Using (
9.38
) and Hölder’s inequality, we get

\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},dh_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},dh_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq C(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}(1+M_{0}).

By (
9.23
), this yields

\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},dh_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq 2.

By using Newton-Leibniz’s formula and (
9.6
), it follows that

\|\mathbf{v}_{j},h_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}}\leq|\mathbf{v}_{0j},h_{0j}|+\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},dh_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq 2+C_{0}.

Using the energy theorem
3.14
, we get

E(T^{*}_{N_{0}})\leq C(1+M_{0})^{5}\exp\{C(1+M_{0})^{3}\}.

Case 2:
j\geq N_{0}+1
.
In this case, we expect to extend the time interval
I_{1}=[0,T^{*}_{j}]
to
[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]
. Our idea is to use (
9.43
), (
9.59
), and Theorem
3.14
to calculate the energy at time
T_{j}^{*}
. Starting at
T_{j}^{*}
, we can also get a new time-interval.

We set

I_{1}=[0,T^{*}_{j}]=[t_{0},t_{1}],\quad\quad|I_{1}|=(CM_{0})^{-3}2^{-\delta_{1}j}.

By using frequency decomposition, we get

\begin{split}d\mathbf{v}_{j}=&\textstyle{\sum}^{\infty}_{k=j}d\mathbf{v}_{j}+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j}\\
=&P_{\geq j}d\mathbf{v}_{j}+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}_{m=k}^{j-1}P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m})+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k}.\end{split}

(9.64)

Similarly, we also have

\begin{split}dh_{j}=&P_{\geq j}dh_{j}+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}_{m=k}^{j-1}P_{k}(dh_{m+1}-dh_{m})+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}P_{k}dh_{k}.\end{split}

(9.65)

When
k\geq j
, using (
9.60
) and (
9.62
), we have
6
6

6

In the case
j\geq N_{0}
we use (
9.60
). In the case
j<N_{0}
, we take
T^{*}_{j}=T^{*}_{N_{0}}
and use (
9.62
) to give a bound on
[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]
.

\begin{split}\|P_{k}dh_{j},P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot C(1+M_{0})^{3}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}j}.\end{split}

(9.66)

When
k<j
, using (
9.61
) and (
9.63
), it follows
7
7

7

In the case
m\geq N_{0}
we use (
9.61
). In the case
m<N_{0}
, we take
T^{*}_{m+1}=T^{*}_{N_{0}}
and use (
9.63
) to give a bound on
[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]
.

\|P_{k}d({h}_{m+1}-{h}_{m}),P_{k}d({\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot C(1+M_{0})^{3}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}m}.

(9.67)

We will use and (
9.66
)-(
9.67
) to give a precise analysis of (
9.64
)-(
9.65
) and get some new time-intervals, and then we try to extend
\rho_{j}
from
[0,T^{*}_{j}]
to
[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]
. Our strategy is as follows. In step 1, we extend it from
[0,T^{*}_{j}]
to
[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]
. In step 2, we use induction methods to conclude these estimates and also extend it to
[0,T_{N_{0}}^{*}]
.

Step 1: Extending
[0,T^{*}_{j}]
to
[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]\ (j\geq N_{0}+1)
.
To start, referring (
9.22
), we need to calculate
E(T^{*}_{j})
for obtaining a length of a new time-interval. Then we shall calculate
\|dh_{j},d\mathbf{v}_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}
. Using (
9.64
) and (
9.65
), we derive that

\begin{split}\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},dh_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&\|P_{\geq j}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{\geq j}dh_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k},P_{k}dh_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}_{m=k}^{j-1}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m}),P_{k}(dh_{m+1}-dh_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&\|P_{\geq j}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{\geq j}dh_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k},P_{k}dh_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{k}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}_{m=k}^{j-1}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m}),P_{k}(dh_{m+1}-dh_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(1+M_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}_{k=j}^{\infty}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}j}\\
&+C(1+M_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}k}\\
&+C(1+M_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}_{m=k}^{j-1}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}m}\\
\leq&C(1+M_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}[\frac{1}{3}(1-2^{-\delta_{1}})]^{-2}.\end{split}

we get

\begin{split}\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},dh_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq C(1+M_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}[\frac{1}{3}(1-2^{-\delta_{1}})]^{-2}\leq 2.\end{split}

(9.68)

By using (
9.68
) and (
9.6
), we get

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{v}_{j},h_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq\|\mathbf{v}_{0j},h_{0j}\|+\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},dh_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq C_{0}+2.\end{split}

By (
9.68
), (
9.23
) and Theorem
3.14
, we have

\begin{split}E(T^{*}_{j})\leq C(1+M_{0})^{5}\exp\{C(1+M_{0})^{3}\}=C_{*}.\end{split}

(9.69)

Above,
C_{*}
is stated in (
9.24
). Starting at the time
T^{*}_{j}
, seeing (
9.22
) and (
9.69
), we can obtain an extending time-interval with a length of
(C_{*})^{-3}2^{-\delta_{1}j}
. But, if
T^{*}_{j}+(C_{*})^{-3}2^{-\delta_{1}j}\geq T^{*}_{j-1}
, we have finished this step. Else, we need to extend it again.

Case 1:

T^{*}_{j}+(C_{*})^{-3}2^{-\delta_{1}j}\geq T^{*}_{j-1}
. In this case, we can get a new interval

I_{2}=[T^{*}_{j},T^{*}_{j-1}],\quad|I_{2}|=(2^{\delta_{1}}-1)(CM_{0})^{-3}2^{-\delta_{1}j}.

Moreover, referring (
9.66
) and (
9.67
), we derive

\|P_{k}dh_{j},P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[T^{*}_{j},T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot C(1+C_{*})^{3}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}j},\quad k\geq j,

(9.70)

and
k<j
,

\|P_{k}(d{h}_{j}-d{h}_{j-1}),P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{j}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{j-1})\|_{L^{1}_{[T^{*}_{j},T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot C(1+C_{*})^{3}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}(j-1)}.

(9.71)

Using (
9.23
) and
j\geq N_{0}+1
, (
9.70
) and (
9.71
) yields

\|P_{k}dh_{j},P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[T^{*}_{j},T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot C(1+M_{0})^{3}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}j},\quad k\geq j,

(9.72)

and
k<j
,

\|P_{k}(d{h}_{j}-d{h}_{j-1}),P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{j}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{j-1})\|_{L^{1}_{[T^{*}_{j},T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot C(1+M_{0})^{3}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}(j-1)}.

(9.73)

Therefore, we obtain the following estimate

\begin{split}&\|dh_{j},d\mathbf{v}_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&\|P_{\geq j}dh_{j},P_{\geq j}d\mathbf{v}_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\|P_{k}dh_{k},P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{j}-d\mathbf{v}_{j-1}),P_{k}(dh_{j}-dh_{j-1})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-2}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}_{m=k}^{j-2}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m}),P_{k}(dh_{m+1}-dh_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}.\end{split}

(9.74)

Due to (
9.66
) and (
9.72
), it yields

\begin{split}\|P_{\geq j}dh_{j},P_{\geq j}d\mathbf{v}_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&C(1+M_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{\infty}_{k=j}2^{-\delta_{1}k}(2^{-7\delta_{1}j}+2^{-6\delta_{1}j})\\
\leq&C(1+M_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{\infty}_{k=j}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}j}\times 2.\end{split}

(9.75)

Due to (
9.67
) and (
9.73
), it follows

\begin{split}&\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{j}-d\mathbf{v}_{j-1}),P_{k}(dh_{j}-dh_{j-1})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(1+M_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}2^{-\delta_{1}k}(2^{-6\delta_{1}(j-1)}+2^{-5\delta_{1}(j-1)})\\
\leq&C(1+M_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}(j-1)}\times 2.\end{split}

(9.76)

Inserting (
9.75
)-(
9.76
) into (
9.74
), we derive that

\begin{split}\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},dh_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&C(1+M_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}_{k=j}^{\infty}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}j}\times 2\\
&+C(1+M_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}(j-1)}\times 2\\
&+C(1+M_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}k}\\
&+C(1+M_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-2}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}_{m=k}^{j-2}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}m}\\
\leq&C(1+M_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}[\frac{1}{3}(1-2^{-\delta_{1}})]^{-2}.\end{split}

(9.77)

By using (
9.77
), (
9.23
) and Theorem
3.14
, we can establish

\begin{split}&|\mathbf{v}_{j},h_{j}|\leq 2+C_{0},\\
&E(T^{*}_{j-1})\leq C_{*}.\end{split}

(9.78)

Case 2:

T^{*}_{j}+(C_{*})^{-3}2^{-\delta_{1}j}<T^{*}_{j-1}
. In this case, we record

I_{2}=[T^{*}_{j},t_{2}],\quad|I_{2}|=(C_{*})^{-3}2^{-\delta_{1}j}.

Referring (
9.66
) and (
9.67
), we can derive

\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}dh_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{I_{2}}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot C(1+C_{*})^{3}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}j},\quad k\geq j,

(9.79)

and

\|P_{k}(dh_{j}-dh_{j-1}),P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{j}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{j-1})\|_{L^{1}_{I_{2}}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot C(1+C_{*})^{3}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}(j-1)},\quad k<j.

(9.80)

Similarly, on
I_{1}\cup I_{2}
, we have

\begin{split}\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},dh_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&\|P_{\geq j}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{\geq j}dh_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}L^{\infty}_{x}}+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k},P_{k}dh_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{k}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{j}-d\mathbf{v}_{j-1}),P_{k}(dh_{j}-dh_{j-1})\|_{L^{1}_{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-2}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}_{m=k}^{j-2}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m}),P_{k}(dh_{m+1}-dh_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}L^{\infty}_{x}}.\end{split}

Noting that
I_{1}\cup I_{2}\subseteq T^{*}_{k}
when
k\leq j-1
, we therefore obtain

\begin{split}&\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},dh_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&\|P_{\geq j}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{\geq j}dh_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}L^{\infty}_{x}}+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k},P_{k}dh_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{j}-d\mathbf{v}_{j-1}),P_{k}(dh_{j}-dh_{j-1})\|_{L^{1}_{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-2}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}_{m=k}^{j-2}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m}),P_{k}(dh_{m+1}-dh_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\end{split}

(9.81)

Inserting (
9.66
), (
9.67
) and (
9.79
) and (
9.80
) to (
9.81
), it follows that

\begin{split}\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},dh_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&C(1+M_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}_{k=j}^{\infty}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}j}\times 2\\
&+C(1+M_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}(j-1)}\times 2\\
&+C(1+M_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}k}\\
&+C(1+M_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-2}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}_{m=k}^{j-2}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}m}\\
\leq&C(1+M_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}[\frac{1}{3}(1-2^{-\delta_{1}})]^{-2}.\end{split}

(9.82)

Applying (
9.82
) and Theorem
3.14
, we get

\begin{split}&|\mathbf{v}_{j},h_{j}|\leq 2+C_{0},\\
&E(t_{2})\leq C_{*}.\end{split}

Therefore, we can repeat the process with a length with
(C_{*})^{-1}2^{-\delta_{1}j}
till extending it to
T^{*}_{j-1}
. Moreover, on every new time-interval with
(C_{*})^{-1}2^{-\delta_{1}j}
, the estimates (
9.79
) and (
9.80
) hold. Set

X_{1}=\frac{T^{*}_{j-1}-T^{*}_{j}}{C^{-3}_{*}2^{-\delta_{1}j}}=(2^{\delta_{1}}-1)C^{3}_{*}(CM_{0})^{-3}.

(9.83)

Then we need a maximum of
X_{1}
-times to reach the time
T^{*}_{j-1}
both in case 2 (it’s also adapt to case 1 for calculating the times). As a result, we can calculate

\begin{split}\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},dh_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&\|P_{\geq j}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{\geq j}dh_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k},P_{k}dh_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{k}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
+&\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{j}-d\mathbf{v}_{j-1}),P_{k}(dh_{j}-dh_{j-1})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}.\\
&+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-2}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}_{m=k}^{j-2}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m}),P_{k}(dh_{m+1}-dh_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\end{split}

Due to (
9.66
), (
9.67
), (
9.79
), (
9.80
), and (
9.23
), we obtain

\begin{split}&\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},dh_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(1+CM_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}_{k=j}^{\infty}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}j}\times(2^{\delta_{1}}-1)C^{3}_{*}(CM_{0})^{-3}\\
&+C(1+CM_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}(j-1)}\times(2^{\delta_{1}}-1)C^{3}_{*}(CM_{0})^{-3}\\
&+C(1+CM_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}k}\\
&+C(1+CM_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-2}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}_{m=k}^{j-2}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}m}\\
\leq&C(1+M_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}[\frac{1}{3}(1-2^{-\delta_{1}})]^{-2}.\end{split}

(9.84)

Therefore, by using (
9.84
) and Theorem (
3.14
), we get

\begin{split}&\|\mathbf{v}_{j},h_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}}\leq 2+C_{0},\\
&E(T^{*}_{j-1})\leq C_{*}.\end{split}

(9.85)

At this stage, both in case 1 or case 2, seeing from (
9.85
), (
9.84
), (
9.77
), (
9.78
), through a maximum of
X_{1}=(2^{\delta_{1}}-1)C^{3}_{*}(CM_{0})^{-3}
times with each length
C_{*}^{-3}2^{-\delta_{1}j}
or
(2^{\delta_{1}}-1)(CM_{0})^{-3}2^{-\delta_{1}j}
, we can extend the solutions
(h_{j},\mathbf{v}_{j},\mathbf{w}_{j})
from
[0,T^{*}_{j}]
to
[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]
, and

\begin{split}&\|\mathbf{v}_{j},h_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]\times\mathbb{R}^{2}}}\leq 2+C_{0},\quad E(T^{*}_{j-1})\leq C_{*},\\
&\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},dh_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq C(1+M_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}[\frac{1}{3}(1-2^{-\delta_{1}})]^{-2}.\end{split}

(9.86)

From (
9.86
), we have extended the solutions
(h_{m},\mathbf{v}_{m},\mathbf{w}_{m})
(
m\in[N_{0},j-1]
) from
[0,T^{*}_{m}]
to
[0,T^{*}_{m-1}]
. Moreover, referring (
9.66
) and (
9.67
), (
9.86
), and (
9.83
), we get

\begin{split}&\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{m},P_{k}dh_{m}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{m},P_{k}dh_{m}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{m},P_{k}dh_{m}\|_{L^{1}_{[T^{*}_{m},T^{*}_{m-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(1+CM_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}m}+C(1+C_{*})^{3}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}m}\times(2^{\delta_{1}}-1)C^{3}_{*}(CM_{0})^{-3}\\
\leq&C(1+M_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}m}+C(1+M_{0})^{3}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}m}\times(2^{\delta_{1}}-1).\end{split}

(9.87)

For
k\geq m\geq N_{0}+1
, due to (
9.23
) and (
9.87
), it yields

\begin{split}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{m},P_{k}dh_{m}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&C(1+M_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}m}\times 2^{\delta_{1}},\end{split}

(9.88)

Similarly, if
m\geq N_{0}+1
, using (
9.23
), for
k<j
, the following estimate holds:

\begin{split}&\|P_{k}(d{h}_{m}-d{h}_{m-1}),P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{m}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{m-1})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&\|P_{k}(d{h}_{m}-d{h}_{m-1}),P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{m}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{m-1})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\|P_{k}(d{h}_{m}-d{h}_{m-1}),P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{m}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{m-1})\|_{L^{1}_{[T^{*}_{m},T^{*}_{m-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(1+CM_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}(m-1)}\\
&+C(1+C_{*})^{3}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}(m-1)}\times(2^{\delta_{1}}-1)C^{3}_{*}(CM_{0})^{-3}\\
\leq&C(1+M_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}(m-1)}\times 2^{\delta_{1}}.\end{split}

(9.89)

Step 2: Extending time interval
[0,T^{*}_{j}]
to
[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]
. Based the above analysis in Step 1, we can give a induction by achieving the goal. We assume the solutions
(h_{j},\mathbf{v}_{j},w_{j})
can be extended from
[0,T^{*}_{j}]
to
[0,T^{*}_{j-l}]
through a maximam
X_{l}
times and

\begin{split}X_{l}=&\frac{T_{j-l}^{*}-T_{j}^{*}}{C^{-3}_{*}2^{-\delta_{1}j}}\\
=&\frac{(CM_{0})^{-1}(2^{-\delta_{1}(j-l)}-2^{-\delta_{1}j})}{C^{-3}_{*}2^{-\delta_{1}j}}\\
=&\frac{C^{3}_{*}}{(CM_{0})^{3}}(2^{\delta_{1}l}-1).\end{split}

(9.90)

Moreover, the following bounds

\begin{split}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}dh_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-l}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&C(1+M_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}j}\times 2^{\delta_{1}l},\qquad k\geq j,\end{split}

(9.91)

and

\begin{split}&\|P_{k}(d{h}_{m+1}-d{h}_{m}),P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m-l}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(1+M_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}m}\times 2^{\delta_{1}l},\quad k<j,\end{split}

(9.92)

and

\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},dh_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-l}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq C(1+M_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}[\frac{1}{3}(1-2^{-\delta_{1}})]^{-2}.

(9.93)

and

|\mathbf{v}_{j},h_{j}|\leq 2+C_{0},\quad E(T^{*}_{j-l})\leq C_{*}.

(9.94)

In the following, we will check the estimates (
9.90
), (
9.91
), (
9.92
), (
9.93
), and (
9.94
) hold when
l=1
, and it also holds when we replace
l
by
l+1
.

Using (
9.86
), (
9.88
), (
9.83
), and (
9.89
), then (
9.91
)-(
9.94
) hold by taking
l=1
. Let us now check it for
l+1
. In this case, it implies that
T^{*}_{j-l}\leq T^{*}_{N_{0}}
. Therefore,
j-l\geq N_{0}+1
should hold. Starting at the time
T^{*}_{j-l}
, seeing (
9.22
) and (
9.94
), we shall get an extending time-interval of
(h_{j},\mathbf{v}_{j},w_{j})
with a length of
(C_{*})^{-3}2^{-\delta_{1}j}
. Hence, we can go to the case 2 in step 1, and the length every new time-interval is
C_{*}^{-3}2^{-\delta_{1}j}
. Therefore, the times is

X=\frac{T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}-T^{*}_{j-l}}{C^{-3}_{*}2^{-\delta_{1}j}}=2^{\delta_{1}l}(2^{\delta_{1}}-1)C^{3}_{*}(CM_{0})^{-3}.

(9.95)

Thus, we can deduce that

X_{l+1}=X_{l}+X=(2^{\delta_{1}(l+1)}-1)C^{3}_{*}(CM_{0})^{-3}.

(9.96)

Moreover, for
k\geq j
, we have

\begin{split}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}dh_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}dh_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-l}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}dh_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[T^{*}_{j-l},T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}.\end{split}

Using (
9.66
) and (
9.94
)

\begin{split}&\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}dh_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[T^{*}_{j-l},T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(1+C_{*})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}j}\times 2^{\delta_{1}l}(2^{\delta_{1}}-1)C^{3}_{*}(CM_{0})^{-3},\quad k\geq j.\end{split}

(9.97)

Due to (
9.23
), it yields

\begin{split}(1+C_{*})^{3}C^{3}_{*}(CM_{0})^{-3}(1+CM_{0})^{-3}2^{-\delta_{1}N_{0}}\leq 1.\end{split}

Hence, from (
9.97
) we have

\begin{split}&\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}dh_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[T^{*}_{j-l},T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(1+M_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}j}\times 2^{\delta_{1}l}(2^{\delta_{1}}-1),\quad k\geq j.\end{split}

(9.98)

Using (
9.91
) and (
9.98
), so we get

\begin{split}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}dh_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&C(1+M_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}j}\times 2^{\delta_{1}(l+1)},\qquad k\geq j.\end{split}

(9.99)

If
k<j
, we can derive

\begin{split}&\|P_{k}(d{h}_{m+1}-d{h}_{m}),P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&\|P_{k}(d{h}_{m+1}-d{h}_{m}),P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m-l}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\|P_{k}(d{h}_{m+1}-d{h}_{m}),P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[T^{*}_{m-l},T^{*}_{m-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}.\end{split}

(9.100)

When we extend the solutions
(h_{j},\mathbf{v}_{j},w_{j})
from
[0,T^{*}_{j-l}]
to
[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]
, then the solutions
(h_{m},\mathbf{v}_{m},w_{m})
is also extended from
[0,T^{*}_{m-l}]
to
[0,T^{*}_{m-(l+1)}]
. Seeing (
9.67
) and (
9.94
), we can obtain

\begin{split}&\|P_{k}(d{h}_{m+1}-d{h}_{m}),P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[T^{*}_{m-l},T^{*}_{m-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(1+C_{*})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}m}\times 2^{\delta_{1}l}(2^{\delta_{1}}-1)C^{3}_{*}(CM_{0})^{-3}.\end{split}

(9.101)

Hence, for
m-l\geq N_{0}+1
, using (
9.23
), it yields

(1+C_{*})^{3}C^{3}_{*}(CM_{0})^{-3}(1+CM_{0})^{-3}2^{-\delta_{1}N_{0}}\leq 1.

Based on the above results, (
9.101
) becomes

\begin{split}&\|P_{k}(d{h}_{m+1}-d{h}_{m}),P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[T^{*}_{m-l},T^{*}_{m-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(1+M_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}m}\times 2^{\delta_{1}l}(2^{\delta_{1}}-1).\end{split}

(9.102)

Inserting (
9.92
) and (
9.102
) to (
9.100
), we have

\begin{split}&\|P_{k}(d{h}_{m+1}-d{h}_{m}),P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(1+M_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}m}\times 2^{\delta_{1}(l+1)},\quad k<j,\end{split}

(9.103)

Next, we can bound

\begin{split}&\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},dh_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&\|P_{\geq j}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{\geq j}dh_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=j-l}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{m=k}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m}),P_{k}(dh_{m+1}-dh_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k},P_{k}dh_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
=&\|P_{\geq j}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{\geq j}dh_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=j-l}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k},P_{k}dh_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+1)}_{k=1}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k},P_{k}dh_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{m=j-(l+1)}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m}),P_{k}(dh_{m+1}-dh_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+2)}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}_{m=k}^{j-(l+2)}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m}),P_{k}(dh_{m+1}-dh_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
=&R_{1}+R_{2}+R_{3}+R_{4}+R_{5},\end{split}

(9.104)

where

\begin{split}R_{1}=&\|P_{\geq j}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{\geq j}dh_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}},\\
R_{2}=&\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+2)}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+2)}_{m=k}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m}),P_{k}(dh_{m+1}-dh_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}},\\
R_{3}=&\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{m=j-(l+1)}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m}),P_{k}(dh_{m+1}-dh_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}},\\
R_{4}=&\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+1)}_{k=1}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k},P_{k}dh_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}},\\
R_{5}=&\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=j-l}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k},P_{k}dh_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}.\end{split}

(9.105)

On time-interval
[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]
, we note that there is no growth for
R_{2}
and
R_{4}
in this extending process. For example, considering
R_{2}
, the existing time-interval of
P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m})
is actually
[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]
, and
[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]\subseteq[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]
if
m\geq j-(l+2)
. Therefore, we can use the bounds (
9.66
) and (
9.67
) to handle
R_{2}
and
R_{4}
. While, considering
R_{1},R_{3}
, and
R_{5}
, we need to calculate the growth in Strichartz estimates. Based on this idea, let us give a precise analysis on (
9.105
).

According to (
9.99
), we can estimate
R_{1}
by

\begin{split}R_{1}\leq&C(1+CM_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{\infty}_{k=j}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}j}\times 2^{\delta_{1}(l+1)}.\end{split}

(9.106)

Due to (
9.66
), we have

\begin{split}R_{2}\leq&\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+2)}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+2)}_{m=k}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m}),P_{k}(dh_{m+1}-dh_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}},\\
\leq&C(1+CM_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+2)}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+2)}_{m=k}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}m}.\end{split}

(9.107)

For
1\leq k\leq j-1
and
m\leq j-1
, using (
9.103
), it follows

\begin{split}R_{3}\leq&\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{m=j-(l+1)}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m}),P_{k}(dh_{m+1}-dh_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(1+CM_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{m=j-(l+1)}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}m}\times 2^{\delta_{1}(l+1)}.\end{split}

(9.108)

Due to (
9.66
), it yields

\begin{split}R_{4}\leq&\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+1)}_{k=1}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k},P_{k}dh_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{k}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(1+CM_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+1)}_{k=1}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}k}.\end{split}

(9.109)

If
j-l\leq k\leq j-1
, then
k+l+1-j\leq l
. By using (
9.91
), we can estimate

\begin{split}R_{5}=&\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=j-l}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k},P_{k}dh_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
=&\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=j-l}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k},P_{k}dh_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{k-(k+l+1-j)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(1+CM_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=j-l}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}j}2^{\delta_{1}(k+l+1-j)}.\end{split}

(9.110)

Inserting (
9.106
), (
9.107
), (
9.108
), (
9.109
), (
9.110
) to (
9.104
), it follows

\begin{split}&\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},dh_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(1+CM_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{\infty}_{k=j}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}j}\times 2^{\delta_{1}(l+1)}\\
&+C(1+CM_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+2)}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+2)}_{m=k}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}m}\\
&+C(1+CM_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{m=j-(l+1)}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}m}\times 2^{\delta_{1}(l+1)}\\
&+C(1+CM_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+1)}_{k=1}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}k}\\
&+C(1+CM_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=j-l}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}k}2^{\delta_{1}(k+l+1-j)}\end{split}

(9.111)

In the case of
j-l\geq N_{0}+1
and
j\geq N_{0}+1
, the estimate (
9.111
) yields

\begin{split}&\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},dh_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(1+CM_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}(1-2^{-\delta_{1}})^{-2}\big\{2^{-\delta_{1}j}2^{\delta_{1}(l+1)}+2^{-6\delta_{1}}\\
&\quad+2^{-5\delta_{1}[j-(l+1)]}2^{\delta_{1}(l+1)}+2^{-6\delta_{1}}+2^{-5\delta_{1}(j-l)}2^{\delta_{1}(l+1-j)}\big\}\\
\leq&C(1+CM_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}(1-2^{-\delta_{1}})^{-2}\left\{2^{-6\delta_{1}N_{0}}+2^{-6\delta_{1}}+2^{-5\delta_{1}N_{0}}+2^{-6\delta_{1}}+2^{-6\delta_{1}N_{0}}\right\}\\
\leq&C(1+M_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}[\frac{1}{3}(1-2^{-\delta_{1}})]^{-2}.\end{split}

(9.112)

By using (
9.6
), (
9.23
), (
9.112
), and Theorem
3.14
, we have proved

\begin{split}|\mathbf{v}_{j},h_{j}|\leq 2+C_{0},\quad E(T^{*}_{j-(l+1)})\leq C_{*}.\end{split}

(9.113)

Gathering (
9.96
), (
9.99
), (
9.103
), (
9.112
) and (
9.113
), the estimates (
9.90
)-(
9.94
) hold for
l+1
. Thus, our induction hold (
9.90
)-(
9.94
) for
l=1
to
l=j-N_{0}
. Therefore, we can extend the solutions
(h_{j},\mathbf{v}_{j},\mathbf{w}_{j})
from
[0,T^{*}_{j}]
to
[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]
when
j\geq N_{0}
. We denote

T^{*}=\min\{1,T^{*}_{N_{0}}\}=\min\{1,(CM_{0})^{-3}2^{-\delta_{1}N_{0}}\}.

(9.114)

Taking
l=j-N_{0}
in (
9.93
)-(
9.94
), we therefore get

\begin{split}&E(T^{*})\leq C_{*},\quad\|\mathbf{v}_{j},h_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq 2+C_{0},\\
&\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},dh_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq C(1+M_{0})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}[\frac{1}{3}(1-2^{-\delta_{1}})]^{-2}\leq 2,\end{split}

(9.115)

where
N_{0}
and
C_{*}
(depending on
C_{0},c_{0},s,M_{0}
) are denoted in (
9.23
) and (
9.24
). Similarly, we conclude

\begin{split}\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},dh_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&C(1+M_{0})^{3}[\frac{1}{3}(1-2^{-\delta_{1}})]^{-2}.\end{split}

(9.116)

Due to (
9.114
), (
9.115
), (
9.116
), we have proved (
9.7
), (
9.8
), and (
9.9
).

It still remains for us to prove (
9.10
) and (
9.11
). We present the proof in the following subsection.

9.3.4.
Strichartz estimates of linear wave equation on time-interval
[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]
.

We still expect the behaviour of a linear wave equation endowed with
g_{j}=g(h_{j},\mathbf{v}_{j})
. So we claim a theorem as follows

Proposition 9.3
.

For
s-\frac{3}{4}\leq r\leq\frac{11}{4}
, there is a solution
f_{j}
on
[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]\times\mathbb{R}^{2}
satisfying the following linear wave equation

\begin{cases}\square_{{g}_{j}}f_{j}=0,\\
(f_{j},\partial_{t}f_{j})|_{t=0}=(f_{0j},f_{1j}),\end{cases}

(9.117)

where
(f_{0j},f_{1j})=(P_{\leq j}f_{0},P_{\leq j}f_{1})
and
(f_{0},f_{1})\in H_{x}^{r}\times H^{r-1}_{x}
. Moreover, for
a\leq r-(s-1)
, we have

\begin{split}&\|\left<\nabla\right>^{a-1}d{f}_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq C_{M_{0}}(\|{f}_{0}\|_{{H}_{x}^{r}}+\|{f}_{1}\|_{{H}_{x}^{r-1}}),\\
&\|{f}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}H^{r}_{x}}+\|\partial_{t}{f}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}H^{r-1}_{x}}\leq C_{M_{0}}(\|{f}_{0}\|_{H_{x}^{r}}+\|{f}_{1}\|_{H_{x}^{r-1}}).\end{split}

(9.118)

Proof of Proposition
9.3
.

Firstly, there are some Strichartz estimates for short time intervals. By applying the extension method described in subsection
9.3.2
and summing the short-time estimates corresponding to these intervals, we can derive a specific type of Strichartz estimate that incurs a loss of derivatives. Due to

a+9\delta_{1}\leq r-(s-1)+9\delta_{1}<r-(s-1)+(s-\frac{7}{4})<r-\frac{3}{4},

(9.119)

using (
9.117
), (
9.39
) and (
9.40
), we therefore have

\begin{split}\|\left<\nabla\right>^{a-1+9\delta_{1}}df_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&C(\|f_{0j}\|_{H^{r}_{x}}+\|f_{1j}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}})\\
\leq&C(\|f_{0}\|_{H^{r}_{x}}+\|f_{1}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}}).\end{split}

(9.120)

Next, we will discuss the cases of high frequency and low frequency. For
k\geq j
, which corresponds to the high frequency, we apply Bernstein’s inequality to obtain

\begin{split}\|\left<\nabla\right>^{a-1}P_{k}df_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}=&C2^{-9\delta_{1}k}\|\left<\nabla\right>^{a-1+9\delta_{1}}P_{k}df_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-8\delta_{1}j}\|\left<\nabla\right>^{a-1+9\delta_{1}}df_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}.\end{split}

(9.121)

Combining (
9.120
) and (
9.121
), for
a\leq r-(s-1)
, we obtain

\begin{split}\|\left<\nabla\right>^{a-1}P_{k}df_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&C2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-8\delta_{1}j}(\|f_{0}\|_{H^{r}_{x}}+\|f_{1}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}}),\quad k\geq j.\end{split}

(9.122)

For
k\leq j
, which corresponds to the low frequency, we need to discuss the difference terms. For any integer
m\geq 1
, we have

\begin{cases}\square_{{g}_{m}}f_{m}=0,\quad[0,T^{*}_{m}]\times\mathbb{R}^{2},\\
(f_{m},\partial_{t}f_{m})|_{t=0}=(f_{0m},f_{1m}),\end{cases}

and

\begin{cases}\square_{{g}_{m+1}}f_{m+1}=0,\quad[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]\times\mathbb{R}^{2},\\
(f_{m+1},\partial_{t}f_{m+1})|_{t=0}=(f_{0(m+1)},f_{1(m+1)}).\end{cases}

Then the difference term
f_{m+1}-f_{m}
is satisfied by

\begin{cases}\square_{{g}_{m+1}}(f_{m+1}-f_{m})=({g}^{\alpha i}_{m+1}-{g}^{\alpha i}_{m})\partial^{2}_{\alpha i}f_{m},\quad[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]\times\mathbb{R}^{2},\\
(f_{m+1}-f_{m},\partial_{t}(f_{m+1}-f_{m}))|_{t=0}=(f_{0(m+1)}-f_{0m},f_{1(m+1)}-f_{1m}).\end{cases}

Due to (
9.40
), (
9.119
), and Duhamel’s principle, which yields

\begin{split}&\|\left<\nabla\right>^{a-1}P_{k}(df_{m+1}-df_{m})\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C\|f_{0(m+1)}-f_{0m}\|_{H_{x}^{r-9\delta_{1}}}+C\|f_{1(m+1)}-f_{1m}\|_{H_{x}^{r-1-9\delta_{1}}}\\
&\ +C\|({g}_{m+1}-{g}_{m})\cdot\nabla df_{m}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}H^{r}_{x}}\\
\leq&C2^{-9\delta_{1}m}(\|f_{0}\|_{H^{r}_{x}}+\|f_{1}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}})+C\|{g}_{m+1}-{g}_{m}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\nabla df_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}H^{r-1}_{x}}.\end{split}

(9.123)

By applying energy estimates and the Bernstein inequality, it follows that

\begin{split}\|\nabla df_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}H^{r-1}_{x}}\leq&C\|df_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}H^{r}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(\|f_{0m}\|_{H^{r+1}_{x}}+\|f_{1m}\|_{H^{r}_{x}})\\
\leq&C2^{m}(\|f_{0m}\|_{H^{r}_{x}}+\|f_{1m}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}}).\end{split}

(9.124)

Using Hölder’s inequality, the Strichartz estimates (
9.40
) and Duhamel’s principle, we obtain

\begin{split}&2^{m}\|{g}_{m+1}-{g}_{m}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&2^{m}(T^{*}_{m+1})^{\frac{3}{4}}\|{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m},h_{m+1}-h_{m}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C2^{m}(\|{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m},h_{m+1}-h_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}H^{\frac{3}{4}+\delta_{1}}_{x}}+\|{w}_{m+1}-{w}_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}H^{\delta_{1}}_{x}})\\
\leq&C2^{-8\delta_{1}m}(1+CM_{0})^{3}.\end{split}

(9.125)

Above, we also use (
9.44
), (
9.45
), and (
9.22
). According to (
9.124
) and (
9.125
), for
k<j
and
k\leq m
, (
9.123
) tells us that

\begin{split}\|\left<\nabla\right>^{a-1}P_{k}(df_{m+1}-df_{m})\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq C2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-8\delta_{1}m}\|(f_{0},f_{1})\|_{H^{r}_{x}\times H^{r-1}_{x}}(1+CM_{0})^{3}.\end{split}

(9.126)

Although we have established some estimates for difference terms, we also need to discuss the energy estimates. This is necessary because the key point of the extension method (in subsection
9.3.2
) crucially relies on the uniform bound of the energy. To achieve this goal, we take the operator
\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}
on (
9.117
), which yields the following result:

\begin{cases}\square_{{g}_{j}}\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}f_{j}=[\square_{{g}_{j}},\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}]f_{j},\\
(\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}f_{j},\partial_{t}\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}f_{j})|_{t=0}=(\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}f_{0j},\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}f_{1j}).\end{cases}

(9.127)

To estimate
[\square_{{g}_{j}},\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}]f_{j}
, we will divide it into two cases:
s-\frac{3}{4}<r\leq s
and
s<r\leq\frac{11}{4}
.

Case 1:
s-\frac{3}{4}<r\leq s
.
In this situation, we note that

\begin{split}[\square_{{g}_{j}},\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}]f_{j}=&[{g}^{\alpha i}_{j}-\mathbf{m}^{\alpha i},\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}\partial_{i}]\partial_{\alpha}f_{j}+\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}(\partial_{i}g_{j}\partial_{\alpha}f_{j})\\
=&[{g}_{j}-\mathbf{m},\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}\nabla]df_{j}+\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}(\nabla g_{j}df_{j}).\end{split}

(9.128)

By (
9.128
) and Kato-Ponce estimates, we have
8
8

8

If
r=s
, then
L^{\frac{3}{s-r}}_{x}=L^{\infty}_{x}
.

\|[\square_{{g}_{j}},\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}]f_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\leq C(\|dg_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|df_{j}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}}+\|\left<\nabla\right>^{r}(g_{j}-\mathbf{m})\|_{L^{\frac{2}{1+r-s}}_{x}}\|df_{j}\|_{L^{\frac{2}{s-r}}_{x}})

(9.129)

By Sobolev’s inequality, it follows that

\|\left<\nabla\right>^{r}(g_{j}-\mathbf{m})\|_{L^{\frac{2}{1+r-s}}_{x}}\leq C\|g_{j}-\mathbf{m}\|_{H^{s}_{x}}.

(9.130)

Taking advantage of the Gagliardo-Nirenberg and Young’s inequalities, which yields

\begin{split}\|df_{j}\|_{L^{\frac{3}{s-r}}_{x}}\leq&C\|\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}df_{j}\|^{\frac{3/4}{3/4+(2-s)}}_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\left<\nabla\right>^{r-(s-\frac{3}{4})}df_{j}\|^{\frac{2-s}{3/4+(2-s)}}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(\|df_{j}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}}+\|\left<\nabla\right>^{r-(s-\frac{3}{4})}df_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}})\end{split}

(9.131)

Case 2:
s<r\leq\frac{11}{4}
.
For
s<r\leq\frac{11}{4}
, applying Kato-Ponce estimates, we have

\begin{split}\|[\square_{{g}_{j}},\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}]f_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}=&\|{g}^{\alpha i}_{j}-\mathbf{m}^{\alpha i},\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}]\partial^{2}_{\alpha i}f_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(\|dg_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|df_{j}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}}+\|\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}(g_{j}-\mathbf{m})\|_{L^{\frac{2}{1+s-r}}_{x}}\|\nabla df_{j}\|_{L^{\frac{2}{r-s}}_{x}}).\end{split}

(9.132)

By Sobolev’s inequality, it follows that

\|\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}(g_{j}-\mathbf{m})\|_{L^{\frac{2}{1+s-r}}_{x}}\leq C\|g_{j}-\mathbf{m}\|_{H^{s}_{x}}.

(9.133)

Note that
\frac{7}{4}<s\leq 2
. By applying the Gagliardo-Nirenberg and Young’s inequalities, we derive:

\begin{split}\|\nabla df_{j}\|_{L^{\frac{3}{1+s-r}}_{x}}\leq&C\|\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}df_{j}\|^{\frac{3/4}{3/4+(2-s)}}_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\left<\nabla\right>^{r-(s-\frac{3}{4})}df_{j}\|^{\frac{2-s}{3/4+(2-s)}}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(\|df_{j}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}}+\|\left<\nabla\right>^{r-(s-\frac{3}{4})}df_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}).\end{split}

(9.134)

Therefore, in both cases, for
s-\frac{3}{4}<r\leq\frac{11}{4}
, using (
9.129
)-(
9.131
) and (
9.132
)-(
9.134
), we can derive

\begin{split}\|[\square_{{g}_{j}},\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}]f_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|df_{j}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}}\leq&C\|dg_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|df_{j}\|^{2}_{H^{r-1}_{x}}+C\|g_{j}-\mathbf{m}\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\|df_{j}\|^{2}_{H^{r-1}_{x}}\\
&+C\|g_{j}-\mathbf{m}\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\left<\nabla\right>^{r-\frac{s}{2}-1}df_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|df_{j}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(\|dg_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|g_{j}-\mathbf{m}\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\left<\nabla\right>^{r-(s-\frac{3}{4})}df_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}})\|df_{j}\|^{2}_{H^{r-1}_{x}}\\
&+C\|g_{j}-\mathbf{m}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\left<\nabla\right>^{r-(s-\frac{3}{4})}df_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}.\end{split}

The above estimate, when combined with (
9.127
), yields

\begin{split}\frac{d}{dt}\|df_{j}\|^{2}_{H^{r-1}_{x}}\leq&C(\|dg_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|g_{j}-\mathbf{m}\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\left<\nabla\right>^{r-(s-\frac{3}{4})}df_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}})\|df_{j}\|^{2}_{H^{r-1}_{x}}\\
&+C\|g_{j}-\mathbf{m}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\left<\nabla\right>^{r-(s-\frac{3}{4})}df_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}.\end{split}

Using Gronwall’s inequality, we obtain

\begin{split}\|df_{j}(t)\|^{2}_{H^{r-1}_{x}}\leq&C(\|df_{j}(0)\|^{2}_{H^{r-1}_{x}}+C\int^{t}_{0}\|g_{j}-\mathbf{m}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\left<\nabla\right>^{r-(s-\frac{3}{4})}df_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau)\\
&\qquad\cdot\exp\left\{\int^{t}_{0}\|dg_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|g_{j}-\mathbf{m}\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\left<\nabla\right>^{r-(s-\frac{3}{4})}df_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau\right\}.\end{split}

(9.135)

On the other hand, the Newton-Leibniz formula tells us

\begin{split}\|f_{j}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\leq&\|f_{j}(0)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial_{t}f_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau.\end{split}

(9.136)

Summarizing the results from (
9.135
), (
9.136
) and (
9.115
), for
t\in[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]
, we obtain

\begin{split}&\|f_{j}(t)\|^{2}_{H^{r}_{x}}+\|df_{j}(t)\|^{2}_{H^{r-1}_{x}}\\
\leq&C\textrm{e}^{C_{*}}(\|f_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{r}_{x}}+\|f_{1}\|^{2}_{H^{r-1}_{x}}+C_{*})\\
&\quad\cdot\exp\left\{\int^{t}_{0}\|\left<\nabla\right>^{r-\frac{s}{2}-1}df_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau\cdot\exp\left(\int^{t}_{0}\|\left<\nabla\right>^{r-(s-\frac{3}{4})}df_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau\right)\right\}.\end{split}

(9.137)

For
a\leq r-(s-1)
, based on (
9.122
), (
9.126
), and (
9.137
), and following the extension method outlined in subsection
9.3.2
, we can obtain the following bounds:

\begin{split}\|\left<\nabla\right>^{a-1}P_{k}df_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&C2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-8\delta_{1}j}(\|f_{0}\|_{H^{r}_{x}}+\|f_{1}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}})\times\left\{2^{\delta_{1}(j-N_{0})}\right\}^{\frac{3}{4}}\\
\leq&C2^{-\frac{3}{4}\delta_{1}N_{0}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-4\delta_{1}j}(\|f_{0}\|_{H^{r}_{x}}+\|f_{1}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}}),\quad k\geq j,\end{split}

(9.138)

and

\begin{split}&\|\left<\nabla\right>^{a-1}P_{k}(df_{m+1}-df_{m})\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-8\delta_{1}m}(\|f_{0}\|_{H^{r}_{x}}+\|f_{1}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}})(1+CM_{0})^{3}\times\left\{2^{\delta_{1}(m+1-N_{0})}\right\}^{\frac{3}{4}}\\
\leq&C2^{-\frac{3}{4}\delta_{1}N_{0}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-4\delta_{1}m}(\|f_{0}\|_{H^{r}_{x}}+\|f_{1}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}})(1+CM_{0})^{3},\quad k<j.\end{split}

(9.139)

By decomposing the frequency, we obtain

df_{j}=P_{\geq j}df_{j}+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{m=k}P_{k}(df_{m+1}-df_{m})+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}P_{k}df_{k}.

By using (
9.138
) and (
9.139
), we get

\begin{split}\|\left<\nabla\right>^{a-1}df_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&C(\|f_{0}\|_{H^{r}_{x}}+\|f_{1}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}})(1+CM_{0})^{3}[\frac{1}{3}(1-2^{-\delta_{1}})]^{-2}.\end{split}

(9.140)

Applying (
9.137
) and (
9.140
), we obtain

\begin{split}\|f_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}H^{r}_{x}}+\|df_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}H^{r-1}_{x}}\leq&A_{*}\exp(B_{*}\mathrm{e}^{B_{*}}),\end{split}

where
C_{*}
is as stated in (
9.24
) and

\begin{split}A_{*}=&C\mathrm{e}^{C_{*}}(\|f_{0}\|_{H^{r}_{x}}+\|f_{1}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}}+C_{*}),\\
B_{*}=&C(\|f_{0}\|_{H^{r}_{x}}+\|f_{1}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}})(1+CM_{0})^{3}[\frac{1}{3}(1-2^{-\delta_{1}})]^{-2}.\end{split}

At this stage, we have proved (
9.118
).
∎

10.
Proof of Theorem
2.3
and Theorem
2.4

In this section, we assume the fluid is stiff, with the pressure given by
p(\varrho)=\varrho
. Since Remark
1.2
, we can obtain

c_{s}\equiv 1,\quad\Theta\equiv 1,\quad g=\mathbf{m}.

(10.1)

Furthermore, due to (
1.15
), we can see

W^{\alpha}=\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma}\partial_{\beta}w_{\gamma}.

(10.2)

Therefore, we can derive a better wave-transport formulation than in the polytropic case.

10.1.
New formulations and estimates

Let us prove the following lemma:

Lemma 10.1
.

Let
(h,\mathbf{v})
be a solution of (
1.11
) and
p(\varrho)=\varrho
. Let
\mathbf{w}
be defined in (
1.14
). Then the system (
1.11
) can be written as

\begin{cases}&\square h=\mathrm{e}^{-2h}w^{\beta}w_{\beta}-2\partial_{\alpha}h\partial^{\alpha}h-\mathrm{e}^{-2h}\partial_{\alpha}v^{\beta}\partial^{\alpha}v_{\beta},\\
&\square v^{\alpha}=-W^{\alpha},\\
&v^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}w^{\alpha}=w^{\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}v_{\kappa},\\
&v^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}W^{\alpha}=-\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma}\partial_{\beta}v^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}w_{\gamma}+\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma}\partial_{\beta}w_{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}v^{\kappa}.\end{cases}

(10.3)

Proof.

For
c_{s}\equiv 1
, using (
1.11
), we obtain

\partial_{\kappa}v^{\kappa}=0.

(10.4)

Inserting (
10.4
) into the third equation in (
1.19
), it yields

v^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}w^{\alpha}=w^{\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}v_{\kappa}.

(10.5)

Due to (
1.20
) and (
10.1
), we can derive that

\begin{split}\mathcal{D}=&-\mathrm{e}^{-2h}\partial_{\kappa}v^{\beta}\partial_{\beta}v^{\kappa}-2\partial_{\kappa}h\partial^{\kappa}h\\
=&-\mathrm{e}^{-2h}\partial_{\kappa}v^{\beta}(\partial_{\beta}v^{\kappa}-\partial^{\kappa}v_{\beta})-\partial_{\kappa}v^{\beta}\partial^{\kappa}v_{\beta}-2\partial_{\kappa}h\partial^{\kappa}h\\
=&-\mathrm{e}^{-2h}\epsilon_{\beta}^{\ \ \kappa\alpha}\partial_{\kappa}v^{\beta}w_{\alpha}-\mathrm{e}^{-2h}\partial_{\kappa}v^{\beta}\partial^{\kappa}v_{\beta}-2\partial_{\kappa}h\partial^{\kappa}h\\
=&\mathrm{e}^{-2h}w^{\beta}w_{\beta}-\mathrm{e}^{-2h}\partial_{\alpha}v^{\beta}\partial^{\alpha}v_{\beta}-2\partial_{\alpha}h\partial^{\alpha}h.\end{split}

(10.6)

Similarly, using (
1.21
) and (
10.1
), we have

Q^{\alpha}=0,\quad\alpha=0,1,2.

(10.7)

Applying (
10.1
), (
10.4
) and (
3.28
), it yields

v^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}W^{\alpha}=-\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma}\partial_{\beta}v^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}w_{\gamma}+\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma}\partial_{\beta}w_{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}v^{\kappa}.

(10.8)

Substituting (
10.6
) and (
10.7
) to (
1.19
), and combining with (
10.5
) and (
10.8
), we therefore obtain (
10.3
).
∎

Lemma 10.2
.

Assume
0<\epsilon^{\prime}<\epsilon\leq\frac{1}{8}
. Let
(h,\mathbf{v})
be a solution of (
1.11
) and
p(\varrho)=\varrho
. Let
\mathbf{w}
be defined in (
1.14
). Then we have the following energy inequality

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}\lesssim&\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|^{2}_{{H}^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}}+\int^{t}_{0}\left(\|d\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|d\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{\infty,2}}\right)\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}d\tau+\|\mathbf{w}\cdot d\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}.\end{split}

(10.9)

Proof.

First, we have

\|\mathbf{w}\|_{H^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}=\|\mathbf{w}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}.

(10.10)

Noting
\mathbf{w}=(w^{0},\mathring{\mathbf{w}})
and using elliptic estimates, we obtain

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}=&\|w^{0}\|_{\dot{H}^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}+\|\mathring{\mathbf{w}}\|_{\dot{H}^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}\\
=&\|w^{0}\|_{\dot{H}^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}+\|\textrm{curl}\mathring{\mathbf{w}}\|_{\dot{H}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}+\|\textrm{div}\mathring{\mathbf{w}}\|_{\dot{H}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}.\end{split}

Due to (
3.74
), (
10.1
), and (
10.4
), it yields

\begin{split}\textrm{div}\mathring{\mathbf{w}}=&(v^{0})^{-1}v^{i}\epsilon_{\alpha i0}W^{\alpha}-(v^{0})^{-2}v^{i}v^{j}\partial_{j}w_{i}+(v^{0})^{-2}v^{i}w^{\kappa}\partial_{i}v_{\kappa}+(v^{0})^{-1}w^{\kappa}\partial_{t}v_{\kappa}.\end{split}

The above equation tells us

\begin{split}\|\mathrm{div}\mathring{\mathbf{w}}\|_{H^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}\leq&C\|\mathbf{W}\|_{H^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}+(\frac{|\mathring{\mathbf{v}}|}{v^{0}})^{2}\|\mathring{\mathbf{w}}\|_{H^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}+C\|\mathbf{w}\cdot d\mathbf{v}\|_{H^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}.\end{split}

(10.11)

By using (
10.2
), we can see

\begin{split}\|\mathrm{curl}\mathring{\mathbf{w}}\|_{H^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}\leq&\|\mathbf{W}\|_{H^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}.\end{split}

(10.12)

Taking advantage of (
10.11
) and (
10.12
), we get

\left\{1-(\frac{|\mathring{\mathbf{v}}|}{v^{0}})^{2}\right\}\|\mathring{\mathbf{w}}\|_{\dot{H}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}\lesssim\|\mathbf{W}\|_{H^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}\cdot d\mathbf{v}\|_{H^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}.

(10.13)

By use of (
1.10
), then (
10.13
) becomes

\|\mathring{\mathbf{w}}\|_{\dot{H}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}\lesssim\|\mathbf{W}\|_{H^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}\cdot d\mathbf{v}\|_{H^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}.

(10.14)

By using (
3.73
), (
10.1
) and (
10.4
), we obtain

\begin{split}\partial_{i}w_{0}=&\epsilon_{\alpha i0}\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma}\partial_{\beta}w_{\gamma}-(v^{0})^{-1}v^{j}\partial_{j}w_{i}+(v^{0})^{-1}w^{\kappa}\partial_{i}v_{\kappa}-(v^{0})^{-1}w_{i}\partial^{\kappa}v_{\kappa}\\
=&\epsilon_{\alpha i0}W^{\alpha}-(v^{0})^{-1}v^{j}\partial_{j}w_{i}+(v^{0})^{-1}w^{\kappa}\partial_{i}v_{\kappa}.\end{split}

(10.15)

Due to (
10.15
) and (
10.14
), we can derive

\begin{split}\|w^{0}\|_{\dot{H}^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}\lesssim&\|\mathbf{W}\|_{H^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}+\|\mathring{\mathbf{w}}\|_{H^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}\cdot d\mathbf{v}\|_{H^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}\\
\lesssim&\|\mathbf{W}\|_{H^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}\cdot d\mathbf{v}\|_{H^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}.\end{split}

(10.16)

Gathering (
10.10
), (
10.14
), and (
10.16
), we get

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{w}\|_{{H}^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}\lesssim&\|\mathbf{w}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{W}\|_{H^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}\cdot d\mathbf{v}\|_{H^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}.\end{split}

(10.17)

Multiplying
w^{\alpha}
on (
10.5
), integrating it on
[0,t]\times\mathbb{R}^{2}
, and summing
\alpha
over
0
to
2
, it yields

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim&\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\int^{t}_{0}\left(\|(v^{0})^{-1}d\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|(v^{0})^{-2}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\right)\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau\\
\lesssim&\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\int^{t}_{0}\|d\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau.\end{split}

(10.18)

Operating
\Lambda_{x}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}
on the equation (
10.8
), we have

\begin{split}\partial_{t}\Lambda_{x}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}W^{\alpha}+(v^{0})^{-1}v^{i}\partial_{i}\Lambda_{x}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}W^{\alpha}=&-[\Lambda_{x}^{\epsilon^{\prime}},(v^{0})^{-1}v^{i}\partial_{i}]W^{\alpha}-\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma}\Lambda_{x}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}\left\{(v^{0})^{-1}\partial_{\beta}v^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}w_{\gamma}\right\}\\
&+\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma}\Lambda_{x}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}\left\{(v^{0})^{-1}\partial_{\beta}w_{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}v^{\kappa}\right\}.\end{split}

(10.19)

Multiplying
\Lambda_{x}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}W^{\alpha}
on (
10.19
), integrating it on
[0,t]\times\mathbb{R}^{2}
, and summing
\alpha
over
0
to
2
, we can derive

\begin{split}\|\Lambda_{x}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}\mathbf{W}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim&\|\Lambda_{x}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}\mathbf{W}_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\int^{t}_{0}\|d\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\Lambda_{x}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}\mathbf{W}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}\|[\Lambda_{x}^{\epsilon^{\prime}},(v^{0})^{-1}v^{i}\partial_{i}]\mathbf{W}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\Lambda_{x}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}\mathbf{W}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}\|\Lambda_{x}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}(d\mathbf{v}\cdot d\mathbf{w})\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\Lambda_{x}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}\mathbf{W}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau.\end{split}

(10.20)

By using Lemma
3.7
and
3.10
, we can bound the estimate (
10.20
) by

\begin{split}\|\Lambda_{x}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}\mathbf{W}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim&\|\Lambda_{x}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}\mathbf{W}_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\int^{t}_{0}\|d\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\Lambda_{x}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}\mathbf{W}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}\|d\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{\infty,2}}\|d\mathbf{w}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\Lambda_{x}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}\mathbf{W}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}\|d\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|d\mathbf{w}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\Lambda_{x}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}\mathbf{W}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau.\end{split}

This implies

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{W}\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}\lesssim&\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|^{2}_{{H}^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}}+\int^{t}_{0}\left(\|d\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|d\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{\infty,2}}\right)\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}d\tau.\end{split}

(10.21)

Combining with (
10.17
), (
10.18
), and (
10.21
), we therefore get

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}\lesssim&\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|^{2}_{{H}^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}}+\int^{t}_{0}\left(\|d\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|d\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{\infty,2}}\right)\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}d\tau+\|\mathbf{w}\cdot d\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}.\end{split}

∎

We also need the decomposition of the rescaled velocity
\mathbf{v}
. The following lemma can be directly obtained by substituting (
10.1
) and (
10.7
) into (
3.23
).

Lemma 10.3
.

Let
(h,\mathbf{v})
be a solution of (
1.11
) and
p(\varrho)=\varrho
. Let
\mathbf{v}_{+}
and
\mathbf{v}_{-}
be defined in (
1.18
). Then we have

\begin{split}\square\mathbf{v}_{+}=&-2\mathrm{e}^{-2h}(v^{0})^{2}\mathbf{T}\mathbf{T}\mathbf{v}_{-}-\mathbf{v}_{-},\end{split}

(10.22)

where
\mathbf{T}
is stated as in (
1.22
).

10.2.
Proof of Theorem
2.3

We will use bootstrap arguments to prove the existence result stated in Theorem
2.3
. Set

T_{0}=\max\{1,\frac{1}{4C^{2}\big(1+\|h_{0},\mathbf{v}_{0}\|_{H^{\frac{7}{4}+\epsilon}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|_{H^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}}\big)^{8}}\},

(10.23)

where
C
is a universal positive constant. Assume the estimate

\int^{T_{0}}_{0}\left(\|dh,d\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|dh,d\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{\infty,2}}\right)d\tau\leq 1,

(10.24)

holds. By using Lemma
3.13
, for
t\leq T_{0}
we derive that

\begin{split}\|(h,\mathbf{v})\|_{H^{\frac{7}{4}+\epsilon}_{x}}\leq&C\|(h_{0},\mathbf{v}_{0})\|_{H^{\frac{7}{4}+\epsilon}}\exp\left(\int^{t}_{0}\|(dh,d\mathbf{v})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau\right)\\
\leq&C\|(h_{0},\mathbf{v}_{0})\|_{H^{\frac{7}{4}+\epsilon}}.\end{split}

(10.25)

Due to Lemma
10.2
and
0<\epsilon^{\prime}<\epsilon
, when
t\leq T_{0}
, we obtain

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}\leq&C\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|^{2}_{{H}^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}}+C\int^{t}_{0}\left(\|d\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|d\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{\infty,2}}\right)\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}d\tau+C\|\mathbf{w}\cdot d\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}\\
\leq&C\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|^{2}_{{H}^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}}+C\int^{t}_{0}\left(\|d\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|d\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{\infty,2}}\right)\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}d\tau+C\|d\mathbf{v}\cdot d\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}\\
\leq&C\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|^{2}_{{H}^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}}+C\int^{t}_{0}\left(\|d\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|d\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{\infty,2}}\right)\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}d\tau+C\|\mathbf{v}\|^{4}_{H^{\frac{7}{4}+\epsilon}_{x}}.\end{split}

(10.26)

Combining (
10.25
) with (
10.26
), we find

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}\leq&C\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|^{2}_{{H}^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}}+C\int^{t}_{0}\left(\|d\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|d\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{\infty,2}}\right)\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}d\tau+C^{4}\mathrm{e}^{4}\|(h_{0},\mathbf{v}_{0})\|^{4}_{H^{\frac{7}{4}+\epsilon}}.\end{split}

(10.27)

Applying Gronwall’s inequality on (
10.27
), it yields

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}\leq&C(C\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|^{2}_{{H}^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}}+C^{4}\|(h_{0},\mathbf{v}_{0})\|^{4}_{H^{\frac{7}{4}+\epsilon}})\exp\left\{\int^{t}_{0}\left(\|d\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|d\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{\infty,2}}\right)d\tau\right\}\\
\leq&C(\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|^{2}_{{H}^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}}+\|(h_{0},\mathbf{v}_{0})\|^{4}_{H^{\frac{7}{4}+\epsilon}}).\end{split}

Taking square on the above inequality, we get

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{w}\|_{{H}^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}\leq&C(\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|_{{H}^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}}+\|(h_{0},\mathbf{v}_{0})\|^{2}_{H^{\frac{7}{4}+\epsilon}}).\end{split}

(10.28)

By using Lemma
3.12
, for
0<\epsilon^{\prime}<\epsilon\leq\frac{1}{8}
, we can demonstrate

\|\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{-}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T_{0}]}H_{x}^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}}+\|\mathbf{v}_{-}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T_{0}]}H_{x}^{2+\epsilon^{\prime}}}\leq C(1+\|(h,\mathbf{v})\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T_{0}]}H^{\frac{7}{4}+\epsilon}})\|\mathbf{w}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T_{0}]}H_{x}^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}},

(10.29)

and

\|\partial_{t}\mathbf{T}\mathbf{v}_{-}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T_{0}]}H_{x}^{\frac{3}{4}+\epsilon}}+\|\mathbf{T}\mathbf{v}_{-}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T_{0}]}H_{x}^{\frac{7}{4}+\epsilon}}\leq C\|\mathbf{w}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T_{0}]}H_{x}^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}}(1+\|h,\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T_{0}]}H_{x}^{\frac{7}{4}+\epsilon}}),

(10.30)

Take advantage of the estimates (
10.25
) and (
10.28
), the bounds for (
10.29
) and (
10.30
) become

\|\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{-}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T_{0}]}H_{x}^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}}+\|\mathbf{v}_{-}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T_{0}]}H_{x}^{2+\epsilon^{\prime}}}\leq C(1+\|h_{0},\mathbf{v}_{0}\|_{H^{\frac{7}{4}+\epsilon}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|_{H^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}})^{3},

(10.31)

and

\|\partial_{t}\mathbf{T}\mathbf{v}_{-}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T_{0}]}H_{x}^{\frac{3}{4}+\epsilon}}+\|\mathbf{T}\mathbf{v}_{-}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T_{0}]}H_{x}^{\frac{7}{4}+\epsilon}}\leq C(1+\|h_{0},\mathbf{v}_{0}\|_{H^{\frac{7}{4}+\epsilon}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|_{H^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}})^{4},

(10.32)

Due to equation (
10.31
) and Sobolev embeddings, we have

\begin{split}\|d\mathbf{v}_{-}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T_{0}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|d\mathbf{v}_{-}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T_{0}]}\dot{B}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{\infty,2}}\leq&C\|d\mathbf{v}_{-}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T_{0}]}H_{x}^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}}\\
\leq&C(1+\|h_{0},\mathbf{v}_{0}\|_{H^{\frac{7}{4}+\epsilon}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|_{H^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}})^{3}.\end{split}

(10.33)

By applying Strichartz estimates to (
10.3
), and since
0<\epsilon^{\prime}<\epsilon\leq\frac{1}{8}
, we can derive

\begin{split}\|d\mathbf{v}_{+}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T_{0}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&C\big(\|\mathbf{v}_{0}\|_{H^{\frac{7}{4}+\epsilon}}+\|d\mathbf{T}\mathbf{v}_{-}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T_{0}]}H_{x}^{\frac{3}{4}+\epsilon}}+\|\mathbf{v}_{-}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T_{0}]}H_{x}^{\frac{3}{4}+\epsilon}}\big)\\
\leq&C\big(\|\mathbf{v}_{0}\|_{H^{\frac{7}{4}+\epsilon}}+\|d\mathbf{T}\mathbf{v}_{-}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T_{0}]}H_{x}^{\frac{3}{4}+\epsilon}}+\|\mathbf{v}_{-}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T_{0}]}H_{x}^{2+\epsilon^{\prime}}}\big)\\
\leq&C(1+\|h_{0},\mathbf{v}_{0}\|_{H^{\frac{7}{4}+\epsilon}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|_{H^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}})^{4}.\end{split}

(10.34)

Similarly, since
0<\epsilon^{\prime}<\epsilon\leq\frac{1}{8}
, we can also obtain

\begin{split}\|d\mathbf{v}_{+}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T_{0}]}\dot{B}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{\infty,2}}\leq&C\big(\|\mathbf{v}_{0}\|_{H^{\frac{7}{4}+\epsilon}}+\|d\mathbf{T}\mathbf{v}_{-}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T_{0}]}H_{x}^{\frac{3}{4}+\epsilon}}+\|\mathbf{v}_{-}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T_{0}]}H_{x}^{\frac{3}{4}+\epsilon}}\big)\\
\leq&C\big(\|\mathbf{v}_{0}\|_{H^{\frac{7}{4}+\epsilon}}+\|d\mathbf{T}\mathbf{v}_{-}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T_{0}]}H_{x}^{\frac{3}{4}+\epsilon}}+\|\mathbf{v}_{-}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T_{0}]}H_{x}^{2+\epsilon^{\prime}}}\big)\\
\leq&C(1+\|h_{0},\mathbf{v}_{0}\|_{H^{\frac{7}{4}+\epsilon}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|_{H^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}})^{4}.\end{split}

(10.35)

Applying the Strichartz estimates on the first equation in system (
10.3
), it yields

\begin{split}\|dh\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T_{0}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|dh\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T_{0}]}\dot{B}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{\infty,2}}\leq&C\big(\|h_{0}\|_{H^{\frac{7}{4}+\epsilon}}+\|(d\mathbf{v},dh)\cdot(d\mathbf{v},dh)\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T_{0}]}H_{x}^{\frac{3}{4}+\epsilon}}\big)\\
\leq&C\big(\|h_{0}\|_{H^{\frac{7}{4}+\epsilon}}+\|d\mathbf{v},dh\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T_{0}]}L_{x}^{\infty}}\|d\mathbf{v},dh\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T_{0}]}H_{x}^{\frac{3}{4}+\epsilon}}\big)\\
\leq&C(1+\|h_{0},\mathbf{v}_{0}\|_{H^{\frac{7}{4}+\epsilon}}),\end{split}

(10.36)

where we also use (
10.24
) and (
10.25
). Due to (
10.33
), (
10.34
), (
10.35
), (
10.36
), and Hölder’s inequality, we can prove

\begin{split}\int^{T_{0}}_{0}\big(\|dh,d\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|dh,d\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{\infty,2}}\big)d\tau\leq&T_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(\|dh,d\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T_{0}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|d\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T_{0}]}\dot{B}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{\infty,2}}\big)\\
\leq&T_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(\|d\mathbf{v}_{-}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T_{0}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|d\mathbf{v}_{-}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T_{0}]}\dot{B}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{\infty,2}}\big)\\
&+T_{0}^{\frac{1}{4}}\big(\|dh,d\mathbf{v}_{+}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T_{0}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|dh,d\mathbf{v}_{+}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T_{0}]}\dot{B}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{\infty,2}}\big)\\
\leq&CT_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(1+\|h_{0},\mathbf{v}_{0}\|_{H^{\frac{7}{4}+\epsilon}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|_{H^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}}\big)^{4}.\end{split}

Noting (
10.23
), we therefore get

\int^{T_{0}}_{0}\big(\|dh,d\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|dh,d\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{\infty,2}}\big)d\tau\leq\frac{1}{2}.

According to bootstrap arguments, the estimate (
10.24
) holds. Therefore, on
[0,T_{0}]\times\mathbb{R}^{2}
, the total energy satisfies

\begin{split}\|h\|_{H_{x}^{\frac{7}{4}+\epsilon}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|_{H_{x}^{\frac{7}{4}+\epsilon}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|_{{H}^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}_{x}}\leq&C\big(1+\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|_{{H}^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}}+\|h_{0}\|_{H^{\frac{7}{4}+\epsilon}}+\|\mathbf{v}_{0}\|_{H^{\frac{7}{4}+\epsilon}}\big)^{2}.\end{split}

Moreover, the following Strichartz estimate holds:

\begin{split}\|dh,d\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T_{0}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|dh,d\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T_{0}]}\dot{B}^{\epsilon^{\prime}}_{\infty,2}}\leq&C\big(1+\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|_{{H}^{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}}+\|h_{0}\|_{H^{\frac{7}{4}+\epsilon}}+\|\mathbf{v}_{0}\|_{H^{\frac{7}{4}+\epsilon}}\big)^{4}.\end{split}

We thus prove the existence of solutions. For uniqueness and continuous dependence, we omit the details because the system (
10.3
) is semi-linear and straightforward to verify. At this stage, we have proved Theorem
2.3
.

10.3.
Proof of Theorem
2.4

For the system (
2.8
) satisfying the “wave map” null condition, we refer to Klainerman and Selberg
[
39
]
(Theorem 1) and Tataru
[
72
]
(page 38, Theorem 1). Consequently, we obtain the conclusions stated in Theorem
2.4
.
